# Rift Time
Categories: Player Statistics, Mechanics
[Rift Time]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the amount of time one can spend in the Rift ([[Rift Dimension]]) on every visit.
Increasing: [[Armor]], [[Accessories]], [[Porhtal]], Slayers ([[Slayer]]) and more
[Values]
Base Value: 480
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E020
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Clock ()
Rift Time is a [[Stats|Rift Stat]]. It determines the amount of time the player can spend in the [[Rift Dimension]] before being sent back to the [[Wizard Tower]] in the [[Hub Island|Hub]].
## Mechanics
The player has a base Rift Time of 8:00. Each Rift Time the player has is equivalent to each second the player has left in the [[Rift Dimension]]. While the player is in the [[Wizard Tower (Rift)|Wizard Tower]], [[Rift Gallery]], or [[Mirrorverse]], their Rift Time does not deplete. When in or near the [[Colosseum (Rift)|Colosseum]], it depletes two times slower, and when in the [[Time Chamber]], it depletes two times faster.
Once the player's Rift Time drops to 00:10, they will be alerted with the following message:
-----------------------------------------------------
THE RIFT IS COLLAPSING
You only have 10 seconds left before you are sent back
to the Wizard in the SkyBlock Hub!
-----------------------------------------------------
When the player's Rift Time reaches 0s, they will be removed from the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]], and will be teleported to the [[Wizard Tower]] on the [[Hub Island]].
The last 10s of Rift Time is immune to any effects that removes your Rift Time.
## Increasing Base Rift Time
|Name|Remarks||
|-|-|-|
|[[McGrubber's Burger]]|Can be eaten a maximum of 5 times.|+12 → +60|
|[[Reed]]|Steps #1 and #2 grant +20 once completed. Steps #3 to #5 grant +30 .|+0 → +130|
|Porhtal's Eyes ([[Porhtal]])|Each eye defeated grants Rift Time.|+0 → +280|
|[[Vampire Slayer]]|Vampire Slayer levels grants 20, 30, 45, 60 and 80 Rift Time respectively.|+0 → +235|
|Sun Gecko Essence Shop ([[Sun Gecko]])|The Blessing of Time in the Sun Gecko Essence Shop grants +30 each tier.|+0 → +90|
|Spotlite ([[Discrite]])|Can be consumed to gain a permanent bonus up to 5 times.|+25 → +125|

## Trivia
- The symbol for Rift Time used to be the Cyrillic letter ф (Ef), which was derived from, looks very similar to, and is pronounced the same as the Greek letter φ (Phi). However, they are not the same, and they have different Unicode code points.
- Due to Rift Time not decreasing when in the [[Wizard Tower (Rift)|Wizard Tower]], [[Rift Gallery]], and [[Mirrorverse]], and the lack of naturally hostile mobs in those areas, it is possible to remain in the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]] for an indefinite amount of time.

# Runecrafting Wisdom
Categories: Player Statistics
[Runecrafting Wisdom]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the Runecrafting XP gain.
Increasing: [[Booster Cookie]], and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Symbol: ☯
Unicode Code Point: U+262F
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Book and Quill ()
Runecrafting Wisdom is one of the player's [[Stats|wisdom stats]]. It increases the amount of [[Runecrafting|Runecrafting XP]] the player gains.
## Mechanics
[[Runecrafting|Runecrafting XP]] gained by the player is multiplied by 1+RunecraftingWisdom100. For example, if a player with 20 Runecrafting Wisdom gained +100 [[Runecrafting|Runecrafting XP]], they would gain 1.2x that amount, or +120 [[Runecrafting|Runecrafting XP]].
## Increasing Bonus Runecrafting Wisdom
### Consumables
Certain items can be consumed to grant Runecrafting Wisdom.
|Slot|Item|Rarity|Type||
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Booster Cookie]]|[[Booster Cookie]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Consumable|+25|
### Modifiers
Certain modifiers can be applied to items to grant Runecrafting Wisdom. The items with the modifiers applied to must be used to take effect.
#### Enchantments
Certain [[Enchantments]] can be applied to items via an [[Enchantment Table]] or [[Anvil]] to grant Runecrafting Wisdom. The items with the [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] applied must be used to take effect.
|Icon|Item|Rarity|Type|Applicable||
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Quantum]]|[[Quantum]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])|Necklace ([[Necklaces]])|+1.2|
|[[Quantum]]|[[Quantum]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])|Necklace ([[Necklaces]])|+1.6|
|[[Quantum]]|[[Quantum]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])|Necklace ([[Necklaces]])|+2|
|}
### Maximizing Runecrafting Wisdom
- [[Booster Cookie]]
- Any [[Necklaces|Necklace]] with [[Quantum|Quantum V]] + Weekend
## Notes
- Mayor [[Derpy]] gives a 50% bonus to all Skill experience, this however does not affect the Wisdom Stats.
  - Events granting Skill Experience boost are multiplicative with the "MOAR SKILLZ!!!" perk, and also don't affect Runecrafting Wisdom.
## History

|Aug 27, 2022|Added Runecrafting Wisdom.|

# Sea Creature Chance
Aliases: SCC, Scc, Sea creature chance
Categories: Player Statistics, Outdated pages, Unknown Zone Referenced, Confirmations needed, Fishing, Mechanics
[Sea Creature Chance]
Type: Stat
Uses: Determines the % chance to fish up [[Sea Creatures]]
Increasing: [[Armor]], Baits ([[Bait]]), [[Enchantments]], [[Fishing Rods]], [[Pets]], [[Pet Items]], Reforges ([[Reforging]]) and more
[Values]
Base Value: 20
Max Value: N/A; gives max effect at 100
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E021
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Prismarine Crystals ()
Sea Creature Chance or SCC is one of the player's [[Stats|Fishing Stats]]. It determines the likelihood of a [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] spawning whilst fishing.
## Mechanics
The Sea Creature Chance determines the percentage chance to fish up a [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]]. X Sea Creature Chance means an X% chance of fishing up a Sea Creature. At 100 Sea Creature Chance or above, fishing up a Sea Creature is guaranteed.
Players have a base 20 Sea Creature Chance, which can be increased in different ways.
Sea Creature Chance is divided by 4 while on the [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]] (except on [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]]).

## Achieving Maximum Sea Creature Chance
This section needs to be reworked as its content is outdated. You can help the wiki by editing this page and reworking it.
This article was last edited by Cubingisthebest (contribs|logs|block) on 6:31, October 27 2026 (8 hours ago)
### Permanent Sources
- Base Value (+20 )
### Non-Permanent Sources
- Holding a [[Hellfire Rod]] that is 10✪ (+16.8 )
  - [[Angler|Angler VI]] and [[Expertise|Expertise X]] (+12 )
  - Recombobulated and reforged to [[Salt Cube|Salty]], [[Hardened Wood|Stiff]], [[Lucky Dice|Lucky]], or [[Rusty Anchor|Treacherous]] (+7 )
- Wearing a full set of recombobulated [[Magma Lord Armor]], that has [[Deep Sea Orb|Submerged]] reforge and each piece being 10✪ (+25.6 )
- Wearing a recombobulated [[Magma Lord Necklace]] that has [[Terry's Snowglobe|Snowy]] reforge (+3.5 )
  - With the full set of Magma Lord being in use, this grants an additional (+1.5 ) from its Lord's Blessing perk.
- Wearing a recombobulated [[Backwater Cloak]] that has [[Terry's Snowglobe|Snowy]] reforge (+1.4 )
- Wearing a recombobulated [[Backwater Belt]] that has [[Terry's Snowglobe|Snowy]] reforge (+1.4 )
- Wearing a recombobulated [[Backwater Gloves]] that has [[Terry's Snowglobe|Snowy]] reforge (+1.4 )
- Using a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Megalodon Pet]] equipped with a [[Hephaestus Souvenir]] (+20 )
  - During a [[Fishing Festival]], this pet's base stats double, granting an additional (+20 )
- In Jerry’s Workshop during a [[Weather|Blizzard]] (+10 )
  - [[Blizzard in a Bottle Mixin]] (+1 )
- [[Attributes|Creature Fisher]] attribute (+5 )
- [[Odger's Tooth|Odger's Diamond Tooth]] (+2 )
- [[Emperor's Artifact]] (+1.5 )
- [[Sea Creature Artifact]] (+0.3 )
- [[Seal Ring]] (+1 )
- [[Century Cakes|Sea Emperor Century Cake]] (+1 )
- Applying a [[Enrichments|Sea Creature Chance Enrichment]] onto 68 talismans (+20.4 )
- Selecting Sea Creature Chance on a Tier 5 [[Beacon]] and using a [[Scorched Power Crystal]] (+6 )
- [[Marina|Mayor Marina]] Luck of the Sea 2.0 (+15 )
- Having [[Bobbin' Time|Bobbin' Time V]] on each piece of [[Magma Lord Armor]] along with 5 bobbers active within a 30 block radius (+20% )
#### Non-Boostable Sources
These are sources that won't increase Sea Creature Chance with the use of [[Bobbin' Time|Bobbin' Time V]]:
- [[Spiked Bait]] (+6 )
- Fishing inside of a [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspot]] that grants Sea Creature Chance (+5 )
  - This can be further increased by using a [[Hotspot Sinker]], granting an additional (+5 )
In total, it is possible to achieve up to 248.56 Sea Creature Chance. [Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
## Tips
- While it is easily possible to achieve over 100 Sea Creature Chance, doing so is not practical since it does not boost your rates. It is more important to favor [[Double Hook Chance]] and [[Fishing Speed]].
- During [[Marina]]'s mayorship, utilizing her perk Double Trouble, it can be more important to have more Sea Creature Chance to receive more [[Double Hook Chance]].
## Trivia
- Considering [[Bait|Fishing Bait]] and [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspots]] do not directly increase your Sea Creature Chance within the SkyBlock Menu, these buffs will not directly increase the [[Double Hook Chance]] granted by [[Marina|Mayor Marina]].
- With a maximum possible value of 218.28 Sea Creature Chance in your SkyBlock Menu, during [[Marina|Mayor Marina]], Double Trouble grants an additional 21.828 [[Double Hook Chance]]. [Confirm]
- An additional 4.2 Sea Creature Chance can be earned if a player uses a weapon with [[Chimera|Chimera V]], granting them 40 Sea Creature Chance. Bringing the new total to 239.32 Sea Creature Chance. [Confirm]
- Unlike all other stats with Chance in their name, Sea Creature Chance, [[Crit Chance]] and [[Bonus Pest Chance]] have no cap.
- The effects of Sea Creature Chance can be nullified using [[Treasure Hook]], which also negates the effects of [[Trophy Chance]] and [[Double Hook Chance]]. Interestingly, it doesn't affect the amount of the stat in any way.
  - The effects of [[Trophy Chance]] are also negated via [[Tera Shell Necklace]].
  - Similarly, [[Lava Shell Necklace]] sets the player's [[Mending]] and [[Health Regen]] to 0, without disabling them.
  - [[Bonus Pest Chance]] can be disabled by the player using [[Pesthunter Badge]] and its upgrades.

# Social Wisdom
Categories: Player Statistics
[Social Wisdom]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the Social XP gain.
Increasing: [[Booster Cookie]], and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Symbol: ☯
Unicode Code Point: U+262F
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Book and Quill ()
Social Wisdom is one of the player's [[Stats|wisdom stats]]. It increases the amount of [[Social|Social XP]] the player gains.
## Mechanics
[[Social|Social XP]] gained by the player is multiplied by 1+SocialWisdom100. For example, if a player with 20 Social Wisdom gained +100 [[Social|Social XP]], they would gain 1.2x that amount, or +120 [[Social|Social XP]].
## Increasing Bonus Social Wisdom

|[[Century Party Invitation]]|[[Century Party Invitation]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Consumable after meeting requirements|+1|

|[[Mountain Goat Shard]]|Social Wisdom ([[Attributes]])|[[Mountain Goat Shard]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|+0.5 → +5|
|[[Wyvern Shard]]|Echo of Wisdom ([[Attributes]])|[[Wyvern Shard]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Increases the effect of Social Wisdom ([[Attributes]]) by 2%–20%|
|[[Tiamat Shard]]|Echo of Echoes ([[Attributes]])|[[Tiamat Shard]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Increases the effect of Echo of Wisdom ([[Attributes]]) by 5%–50%|

# Soulflow (Mechanic)
Aliases: Soulflow, Soulflow (mechanic), Soulflow Cost
Categories: Player Statistics
[Soulflow (Mechanic)]
Type: Stat
Uses: Abilities of some items, Overflow Mana ([[Mana]]) Conversion
Increasing: Consuming Soulflow Items: Soulflow ([[Soulflow (Item)]])/ [[Raw Soulflow]]
[Symbol]
Symbol: ⸎
Unicode Code Point: U+2E0E
Soulflow, or Internalized Soulflow, is a player resource similar to [[Mana]]. It is used to fuel powerful abilities from the [[Enderman Slayer]] and to generate [[Mana|Overflow Mana]].
## Obtaining
A player can obtain Soulflow by consuming the following Soulflow Items:
|Item|Soulflow Granted|
|-|-|
|[[Raw Soulflow]]|1|
|Soulflow ([[Soulflow (Item)]])|160|
A minion will start producing Soulflow Items when equipped with the following Minion Upgrades:
|Item|Remarks|
|-|-|
|[[Lesser Soulflow Engine]]|Produces 50% less; Produces [[Raw Soulflow]] (Also Soulflow ([[Soulflow (Item)]]) with [[Super Compactor 3000]]/ [[Dwarven Super Compactor]])|
|[[Soulflow Engine]]|Produces 50% less; Produces [[Raw Soulflow]] (Also Soulflow ([[Soulflow (Item)]]) with [[Super Compactor 3000]]/ [[Dwarven Super Compactor]]); +3% output when used on a [[Voidling Minion]]|
By consuming Soulflow Items, a player receives an amount of Internalized Soulflow, which is also signified by the icon . Once consumed, there is no way to turn the Soulflow back into items.
There is no known cap for the amount of Internalized Soulflow a player can have at once.
## Usage
Internalized Soulflow is not displayed on the action bar, and can only be viewed on the tooltip of [[Soulflow Pile]] line accessories or in the Profile widget in the [[Settings|Tablist Widgets]].
Certain powerful abilities and items use Soulflow:
|Item|Soulflow Cost|
|-|-|
|[[Handy Blood Chalice]]|1 per 5s in combat|
|[[Pocket Espresso Machine]]|1 per 5s in combat|
|[[Soulflow Pile]]|Up to 5 when leaving combat|
|[[Soulflow Battery]]|Up to 4 when leaving combat|
|[[Soulflow Supercell]]|Up to 4 when leaving combat|
|[[Etherwarp Conduit]]|1|
|[[Voidedge Katana]]|1|
|[[Vorpal Katana]]|1|
|[[Atomsplit Katana]]|2|
|[[Final Destination Armor]]|2 per 5s in combat|
|[[Gloomlock Grimoire]]|15|
|[[Gyrokinetic Wand]]|10 (Left Click)|
|[[Gyrokinetic Wand]]|2 (Right Click)|
|[[Soul Esoward]]|10|
|[[Terminator]]|1|
If insufficient Soulflow is available, the abilities will not work.
## Game Message
|Condition|Game Message|
|-|-|
|Attempting to use an ability; without sufficient Soulflow|Not enough soulflow!|
|Attempting to use an ability; without sufficient Soulflow|nOt EnOuGh SoUlFlOw!|
## Trivia
- The symbol for Soulflow, U+2E0E ⸎ "EDITORIAL CORONIS", is displayed as a lined diamond on some fonts and systems and a square root symbol on other fonts and systems.
- The probability to receive the message "nOt EnOuGh SoUlFlOw!" instead of "Not enough soulflow!" is 2% (1 in 50).

|May 31, 2021|0.11.5|Added Soulflow.|

# Speed
Aliases: Speed Cap
Categories: Player Statistics, Outdated pages, Confirmations needed, Pages with math equations, Mechanics
[Speed]
Type: Stat
Uses: Determines player's speed.
Increasing: [[Armor]], [[Pets]], [[Accessory Power]], [[God Potion]] and more
[Values]
Base Value: 100
Max Value: 400, can be increased
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E022
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Sugar ()
Speed (Spd) is one of the core player [[Stats]]. It is a measure of the walking and running speed of the player expressed as a percentage of the original speed.
## Mechanics
Speed is a measure of how much faster the player walks compared to the default speed. It is expressed as a percentage of the default speed.
Speed is capped at 400% normally, more details below.
Movement speed in blocks per second can be calculated using this formula:
Movement Speed=Base Speed×Speed Modifier
- Base Speed - amount of movement speed coded into Minecraft. Base Speed values for different actions are following:
|Action|Base Speed|
|-|-|
|Sneaking (×0.3)|1.295 blocks/s|
|Walking|4.317 blocks/s|
|Sprinting (×1.3)|5.612 blocks/s|
Speed Modifier - your Speed stat value. Percent value must be converted to decimal (e.x. 230% = 2.3).
Speed does not apply when airborne. Flying speed is always approximately 10.889 blocks/s (252%), or 21.778 blocks/s (504%) when sprinting, after reaching maximum velocity, and assuming you are moving straight forward at a perfect 90 degree angle and not holding any other movement keys.
### Example
Information:
- Action: Sprinting
- Speed value: 207%
Calculation:
5.612×2.07=11.617 blocks/s
## Increasing Base Speed
Players have a base Speed of 100 by default. It can be increased by:
- Leveling [[Wolf Slayer]] (up to 3 Speed)
- Buying Forbidden Speed in the [[Essence Shops|Wither Essence Shop]]. (Up to 2 Speed)

## Speed Cap
Players have a maximum Speed of 400% by default. It can be increased by:
- [[Young Dragon Armor]]'s Full Set ability or equipping a [[Racing Helmet]] (+100 Speed Cap)
- A [[Black Cat Pet]]'s Hunter ability (+1-100 Speed Cap)
- Using the [[Unalloyed Speed]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] (+50 Speed Cap)
- [[Resistance Leader|Mayor Technoblade]] (Removed Speed Cap, though this perk was bugged)
- [[Cactus Knife]] (+100 Speed cap while on the [[The Garden|Garden]])
- [[Folf Shard]] (+5-50 Speed cap while in the [[Catacombs]])
It can also be decreased by:
- [[Rarity|RARE]] or higher [[Snail Pet]] (sets the player's Speed Cap to 100 and gives them 1 [[Block Fortune]] for every 5.97–2 Speed lost)
- [[Cold]] (Every 25 [[Cold]] reduces the Speed Cap by 25% regardless of [[Cold Resistance]])
- Players may use [[Rancher's Boots]] to cap their Speed at a lower value.
- Players may use Crop Speed settings on [[The Garden]] to cap their Speed when breaking a specific crop after giving [[Sam]] a [[Sundial]].
## Decreasing Speed
- A [[Slowness Potion]] decreases Speed by the same intervals as speed potions.
- The [[Venomous Potion]] decreases Speed by 5% per level up to 20%.
- The [[Warden Helmet]] halves Speed after applying the Speed Cap, but grants +20% base weapon damage for every 25 Speed remaining
- The [[Bluetooth Ring]] decreases Speed by 2.
- The [[Bluertooth Ring]] decreases Speed by 3.
- In [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]], any Speed above 100 is reduced by 33%.
## Theoretical Maximum Speed (ignoring Speed Cap)
This article needs to be reworked as its content is outdated.
You can help the wiki by editing this page and reworking it.
Reason: Max MP increase and Foxtrot shard
This article was last edited by ~2026-ArmadilloSalmonFrog38484 (contribs|logs|block) on 10:39, October 18 2026 (8 days ago)
The maximum Speed reachable (bypassing the speed cap) can be reached by using this setup:
- The base Speed the player starts with is 100 Speed
- The [[Rogue Sword]]'s ability activated (take it out of your inventory immediately after use) (+100 Speed)
- Equipping [[Shadow Assassin Armor]] with a [[Recombobulator 3000]], and fragged with the [[End Stone Geode|Hyper]] reforge (+44 Speed)[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] and using an [[Aspect of the End]] (+50 Speed) (take it out of your inventory immediately after use) (+94 Speed)
- Holding a fragged [[Shadow Fury]] with [[Chimera|Chimera V]] ( 44 + 187.5 Speed = +227.5 Speed)
- [[Racing Helmet]] reforged to [[Reforging|Sunny]], [[Recombobulator 3000]] (400 Speed + 10 Speed = +410 Speed)
- [[Obsidian Chestplate]] reforged to [[Reforging|Sunny]], [[Recombobulator 3000]], and having 2,176 (34 stacks) [[Obsidian]] in the Inventory (111 Speed + 10 Speed = +121 Speed)
- [[Terror Armor|Infernal Terror Leggings]] with 15 stars reforged to [[Reforging|Sunny]], and [[Recombobulator 3000]] (49 Speed)
- [[Rancher's Boots]] reforged to [[Reforging|Sunny]], [[Recombobulator 3000]], and enchanted with [[Sugar Rush|Sugar Rush III]] (240 Speed + 10 Speed + 6 Speed = 256 Speed)
- [[Delirium Necklace]] with [[Recombobulator 3000]], 10 stars, and reforged to [[Reforging|Blooming]] (+7.2 Speed)
- [[Zorro's Cape]] reforged to [[Reforging|Blooming]] during a [[Jacob's Farming Contest|Farming Contest]] (+12 Speed)
- [[Snow Belt]] reforged to [[Reforging|Blooming]] (2 Speed + 5 Speed = +7 Speed )
- [[Snow Gloves]] reforged to [[Reforging|Blooming]] (2 Speed + 5 Speed = +7 Speed)
- Equipping a [Lv100] [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Black Cat Pet]] with a [[Hephaestus Relic]] (187.5 Speed + 100 Speed = +287.5 Speed )
- Having [[Accessory Power|2050 Accessory Power]] and having the Buttery Power (+125 Speed)
- Equipping the [[Potato Ring]], [[Speed Relic]], [[Cheetah Talisman]], [[Pig's Foot]], [[Wolf Paw]], [[Frozen Chicken]] and [[Bat Artifact]] (+13 Speed)
- Standing on Galatea with the [[Wood Affinity Talisman]] equipped. (+10 Speed)
- Having the Speed boost [[Heart of the Forest]] perk maxed out (+50 Speed)
- Having 65 Accessories enriched with the [[Enrichments|Speed Enrichment]] (+65 Speed)
- Drinking one of each potion of maximum tier: [[Speed Potion]] with a [[Black Coffee]], [[Adrenaline Potion]] with a [[Black Coffee]], [[Agility Potion]] with a {{Item|Black Coffee}], [[Rabbit Potion]] with a [[Black Coffee]], and a [[Spirit Potion]] (40 Speed + 40 Speed + 40 Speed + 60 Speed + 40 Speed + ( 4 Potions x 12 Speed = 48 Speed ) = 268 Speed)
- Using the [[Weirder Tuba]] (30 Speed)
- Using 218 Tuning Points on Speed (327 Speed)
- Levelling [[Wolf Slayer]] (3 Speed)
- Buying Forbidden Speed in the [[Essence Shops|Wither Essence Shop]] (2 Speed)
- Having the [[Obsidian Defender Shard]] maxed out (10 Speed)
- [[Candidate Jerry|Mayor Jerry]]'s Statspocalyspe perk increases [[Stats|Miscellaneous Stats]] by 10%
This grants a total of (100 + 100 + 44 + 50 + 227.5 + 410 + 121 + 49 + 256 + 7.2 + 12 + 7 + 7 + 287.5 + 125 + 13 + 10 + 50 + 65 + 268 + 30 + 327 + 3 + 2 + 10) * 1.1 =2839.32 Speed. This can be increased with A) Being inside of the [[Catacombs]], or B) Standing on a [[Haste Block]]. However, this Speed is not possible because of the Speed Cap.
## Trivia
- When [[Resistance Leader|Mayor Technoblade]] was elected, the speed cap was supposedly removed, allowing players to achieve speed higher than 600%. However, the real speed of the player was still limited to 500%.
- There used to be a bug where the speed buff from the [[Aspect of the End]] never ran out on the player's island. This allowed for the player to easily achieve 400% speed. This was patched in version 0.7.
- Riding on a [Lv1] [[Skeleton Horse Pet]] is equivalent to walking with 200% Speed (8.62 B/s), and riding on a [Lv100] [[Skeleton Horse Pet]] is equivalent to walking with 500% Speed (21.55 B/s).
- Speed goes under the name speed in the [[SkyBlock API]]. However, items with Speed in the [[Rift Dimension]] have said stat go under the name rift_walk_speed. This means that while both stats act in the same way, they are technically different. Due to this, the speed bonus effect from [[Beacon|Beacons]] does not work in the Rift, while other bonuses for stats such as [[Magic Find]] do.
- While the highest possible speed is 850 Speed, [[Rancher's Boots]]'s can be set to up to 1000.
- The player used to be able to get infinite Speed. This was done by using the [[Bat Person Artifact]] and a glitched chunk to get infinite bats while wearing [[Super Heavy Armor]] or [[Heavy Armor]].
- The player hypothetically could obtain close to 44,000 Speed without bugs or glitches. This would be done by getting the maximum intelligence while holding a [[Rogue Sword]] and then spamming the [[Rogue Sword]] during [[Candidate Jerry|Mayor Jerry]]. This is not possible since a 5s cooldown was added.
- Speed, [[Attack Speed]], [[Ferocity]], [[Swing Range]], [[Mining Spread]], [[Gemstone Spread]], [[Timber]], [[Fishing Speed]], [[Trophy Chance]], [[Treasure Chance]], [[Magic Find]] and [[Charm Chance]] are the only stats with a cap.
  - Of which Speed, [[Attack Speed]] and [[Fishing Speed]] are the only ones whose cap can be changed.
  - Of the above, Speed is the only stat that has an effect even when surpassing the cap, via [[Snail Pet]].

# Speed (Rift)
Aliases: Rift Speed
Categories: Player Statistics, Mechanics
[Rift Speed]
Type: Stat
Uses: Determines player's speed in the Rift Dimension.
Increasing: [[Equipment]], [[Accessories]] and more
[Values]
Base Value: 100
Max Value: 400
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E022
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Sugar ()
[[Speed (Rift)|Rift Speed]] is a [[Stats|Rift Stat]]. It is a measure of the walking and running Speed of the player expressed as a percentage of the original speed in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Mechanics
[[Speed (Rift)|Rift Speed]] is a measure of how much faster the player walks compared to the default speed. It is expressed as a percentage of the default speed.
## Increasing Rift Speed
### Base Rift Speed
The player's base [[Speed (Rift)|Rift Speed]] is 100. It can be increased with the following methods:

### Other Stats
|Stat|Notes|
|-|-|
|[[Effective Health]]|The total amount of [[Health]] the player has when considering the player's [[Defense]].; It is determined by the following formula:; [[Effective Health]]=[[Health]] ×( [[Defense]]/100+1)|
|[[Mana]]|Base value is 100 and increases with the player's [[Intelligence]].|
|[[Vitality]] (resource)|Base value is 100 and increases with the player's [[Vitality]].|
|[[Absorption]]|Same as vanilla Minecraft absorption; it adds extra hearts to your [[Health]] that only last until damage is taken that uses them up.|
|[[True Damage]]|Similar to [[Damage]] except it passes through [[Defense]].|
|[[Heat]]|Gradually increases while in the [[Magma Fields]], once 90 is reached, the player begins to burn and take damage.|
|[[Cold]]|Gradually increases while in the [[Glacite Tunnels]] and 25% faster in the [[Glacite Mineshafts]].; Also increases when taking damage from Glacial ([[Glacial (Mob Type)]]) mobs in [[Glacite Mineshafts]].; Reduces [[Mining Speed]] by 0.5% per [[Cold]] and [[Speed]] cap by 25% per increments of 25 [[Cold]].; Players instantly die after reaching 100 [[Cold]].|

# Strength
Categories: Player Statistics, Confirmations needed, Strength, Mechanics, Pages with math equations
[Strength]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increasing damage dealt.
Increasing: [[Armor]], [[Weapons]], [[Reforging]], [[Accessory Power]], [[Foraging]], [[Pets]] and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E00D
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Blaze Powder ()
Strength (Str) is one of the core player [[Stats]]. It plays a major role in determining the [[Damage Calculation|Final Damage]] dealt by the player.
## Mechanics
Strength increases [[Damage Calculation|Melee Damage]] and [[Damage Calculation|Projectile Damage]].
## Increasing Base Strength
At the start of the game, players have a base Strength of 0. It can be increased by:
- Leveling the [[Foraging]] skill
- Obtaining the Forbidden Strength upgrade in the Wither Essence Shop (from [[Malik]] in the [[Dungeon Hub]])
- Obtaining [[SkyBlock Levels]]

# Sweep
Categories: Player Statistics, Article stubs
[Sweep]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increasing the number of logs broken upon breaking a log.
Increasing: [[Heart of the Forest]], Sweep Booster ([[Common Sweep Booster]]), Agatha's Shop ([[Agatha]]), Tree Milestones ([[Swoop]]), Hunting Milestones ([[David Hunterborough]])
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E023
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Diamond Axe
Sweep is one of the player [[Stats]]. It plays a major role in the [[Foraging]] skill.
## Mechanics
Sweep is one of the statistics in the calculation of how many logs are broken upon breaking a log with an [[Axes|Axe]]. The toughness of different trees makes Sweep less effective. Having a higher Sweep than a tree's toughness is recommended before starting to cut that tree type.
On trees with a [[Toughness]] of 0, Sweep directly corresponds to the number of extra logs broken (capped at 35, for 36 total logs broken). On trees with a higher toughness, amount of logs broken is dependant on the tree and it's [[Toughness]].
Throwing an [[Axes|Axe]] will give a -50% Sweep penalty. This penalty can be reduced with the [[Missile]] enchantment, or removed with the [[Precursor Drone Pet]].
Additionally, on [[Galatea]], breaking trees in the wrong order will sweep another 50% less logs, stacking multiplicatively with the [[Axes|Axe]] throw penalty.
Plus, Sweep also affects the breaking speed of wood, similarly to [[Mining Speed]].
## Formula
### Basic Trees
Trees in the [[Forest]] and [[The Park]] have 0 [[Toughness]]. For these trees, Sweep simply represents the number of extra logs that will be cut, but players will always cut at least 1 and at most 36 logs.
### Galatea Trees
Trees in [[Galatea]] have non-zero [[Toughness]], which is essentially a form of resistance to Sweep. The higher the [[Toughness]], the more Sweep the player needs to cut a given amount of extra logs at once.
The [[Attributes|Chop]] attribute grants the player a 10%–100% chance to cut an additional log.
The amount is capped at a maximum of 20 extra logs cut.

    - Speed beyond the player's speed cap counts towards this perk.

    - maximum [[Mana]]

    - 5 players using the opposite in the server (+50 )

The maximum possible Sweep is 1211.9912
Calculation
flat = 107.1+100+7+102+6+7+42+11.2+10+42+25+25+50+10+10+50+50+37+50+5 = 746.3
multiplier = 1 + (32.4+10+10+10)/100 = 1.624
effective = flat * multiplier = 746.3 * 1.624 = 1211.9912
However, note that there are sources of effective sweep not counted into the stat profile (as listed below). There may also be limitations with the possible number of perks active at once due to limited [[Token of the Forest]]. Attaining maximum 1000 [[Strength]], 500 [[Speed]], and 3749 [[Mana]] to maximize HOTF perks may also be difficult with the equipment used during foraging.
These are sources that won't increase your Sweep stat but will affect your effective sweep when cutting:
- [[First Impression|First Impression V]] (+5 on melee hits and +10 on first hit of each tree)
- [[Attributes]]:
  - [[Crow Shard]] (+50 on Fig trees)
  - [[Heron Shard]] (+100 on Mangrove trees)
  - [[Vulture Shard]] (+150 on Helix trees)
  - [[Gecko Shard]] (+10 on Fig, +20 on Mangrove, +30 on Helix)
  - [[Tiamat Shard]] (+5 on Fig, +10 on Mangrove, +15 on Helix)
  - [[Mochibear Shard]] or [[Bambuleaf Shard]] (+30 )
  - [[Torrid Shard]] [+10% Sweep bonus on first hit of each tree]
- [[Heart of the Forest]]:
  - Early Bird (+20 on the first 250 trees cut every 24h)
  - Precision Cutting (+10 on the log with particles)
When cutting the log marked with a particle, in melee, as the first hit of the tree, and as one of the first 250 logs of the day while using the Maniac Slicer axe ability (and all other requirements listed above), theoretically, one can have 1502.1212 Sweep on Fig logs, 1614.1812 Sweep on Mangrove logs, and 1726.2412 Sweep on Helix logs.
Calculation
multiplier = 1.624 + 10/100 = 1.724
fig: (746.3+50+10+5+30+20+10) * 1.724 = 1502.1212
mangrove: (746.3+100+20+10+30+20+10) * 1.724 = 1614.1812
helix: (746.3+150+30+15+30+20+10) * 1.724 = 1726.2412

# Swing Range
Aliases: Range, Reach
Categories: Player Statistics
[Swing Range]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the distance from which you can hit mobs with melee weapons.
Increasing: [[Longswords]], [[Crimson Armor]], [[Pets]], Berserk Class ([[Berserk]]) and more
[Values]
Base Value: 3
Max Value: 15
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E024
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Stone Sword ()
Swing Range (Swing) is one of the core player [[Stats]]. It is equal to the range at which players can hit enemies with melee weapons in blocks, with a base value of 3.
## Mechanics
Swing Range increases the distance from which you can hit enemies with [[Swords]] and [[Longswords]]. The stat's value is equal to the distance in blocks.
## Increasing Bonus Swing Range
Various [[Longswords]] increase Swing Range. However, even if [[Star Upgrades|Stars]] are applied, the item's Swing Range does not increase.
|Icon|Name|Effect|
|-|-|-|
|[[Bone Reaver]]|[[Bone Reaver]]|Grants +0.5 Swing Range|
|[[Felthorn Reaper]]|[[Felthorn Reaper]]|Grants +1 Swing Range|
|[[Felthorn Reaper]]|[[Felthorn Reaper]]|Grants +1.5 Swing Range|
|[[Giant's Sword]]|[[Giant's Sword]]|Grants +1 Swing Range|
|[[Dark Claymore]]|[[Dark Claymore]]|Grants +2 Swing Range|
|[[Crimson Armor]]|[[Crimson Armor]]|Grants between +0.05 Swing Range and +1 Swing Range, depending on active dominus stacks and armor tier.|
||[[Berserk]] Class in Dungeons|Grants between +0.1 Swing Range per Berserk level, up to +5 Swing Range, doubled to solo berserks.|
|[[Giraffe Pet]]|[[Giraffe Pet]]|+0.01➡1 Swing Range|
|[[Golem Pet]]|[[Golem Pet]]|+0.01➡1 Swing Range|
|[[Blaze Pet]]|[[Blaze Pet]]|+0.1➡10% when on the [[Crimson Isle]]|
|[[Manticore Claw]]|[[Manticore Claw]]|Ability: Chimeric Core Copies 10% of your active pet's Swing Range stats.|
## Maximum Swing Range
- [[Dark Claymore]] (+2.1 Swing Range inside [[Dungeons]] with [[General's Medallion]])
- Berserk level 50 (inside [[Dungeons]] only) (+5 Swing Range, +10 Swing Range for solo Berserks)
- Wearing [[Crimson Armor]] with 10 stacks of Ability: Dominus (+1 Swing Range)
- [[Giraffe Pet]] or [[Golem Pet]] (+1 Swing Range)
  - [[Hephaestus Relic]] (+0.5 Swing Range)
  - [[Chimera|Chimera V]] (+1.5 Swing Range)
- Wearing [[Manticore Claw]] (+0.15 Swing Range)
Doing all of the above, alongside the base value, grants 9.15 Swing Range (14.25 Swing Range inside [[Dungeons]] and 19.25 Swing Range for solo Berserk).
Though, the maximum value for Swing Range is 15.
## Trivia
- Via Swing Range, players can hit mobs that would otherwise not be hittable without double clicking, for example mobs hidden in [[Double Tallgrass]].
- [[Speed]], [[Attack Speed]], [[Ferocity]], Swing Range, [[Mining Spread]], [[Gemstone Spread]], [[Timber]], [[Fishing Speed]], [[Trophy Chance]], [[Treasure Chance]], [[Magic Find]] and [[Charm Chance]] are the only stats with a cap.

# Taming Wisdom

[Taming Wisdom]

Taming Wisdom is one of the player's [[Stats|wisdom stats]]. It increases the amount of [[Taming|Taming XP]] the player gains.
## Mechanics
[[Taming|Taming XP]] gained by the player is multiplied by 1+TamingWisdom100. For example, if a player with 20 Taming Wisdom gained +100 [[Taming|Taming XP]], they would gain 1.2x that amount, or +120 [[Taming|Taming XP]].
## Increasing Bonus Taming Wisdom

# Timber
Categories: Player Statistics
[Timber]
Type: Stat
Uses: Chance to fell the entire tree on your first melee axe cut.
Increasing: Timber, [[Petalfall]], Helix Timber ([[Attributes]])
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: 100
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E02E
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Netherite Axe
  Not to be confused with [[Chocolate Rabbits|Timber]], a Mountain Faction Rabbit.
Timber is a [[Foraging]] [[Stats|stat]]. It increases the chance of felling an entire tree on the first melee axe cut.
## Timber Mechanics
For every 1 Timber the player has, they gain a 1% chance to fell an entire tree on the first melee axe cut. Felling an entire tree causes the [[Sweep]] animation to play until the tree is destroyed and a [[Tree Gifts|Tree Gift]] is rewarded. The player can still continue to cut the tree during the animation.
Timber can be increased through various ways:
|Source|Type|Timber|
|-|-|-|

## Trivia
- It is the only [[Stats|Foraging Stat]] that isn't disabled by default on the player's [[Private Island]].
  - Comparatively, [[Bonus Pest Chance]] is the only [[Stats|Farming Stat]] that isn't disabled by default on the player's [[Private Island]].
- [[Speed]], [[Attack Speed]], [[Ferocity]], [[Swing Range]], [[Mining Spread]], [[Gemstone Spread]], Timber, [[Fishing Speed]], [[Trophy Chance]], [[Treasure Chance]], [[Magic Find]] and [[Charm Chance]] are the only stats with a cap.

# Tracking
Aliases: Traking
Categories: Player Statistics
[Tracking]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the chance to spawn [[Elusive]] mobs
[Values]
Base Value: 0
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+e077
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): [[Compass]]
Tracking is a stat obtained through multiple sources, but primarily by participating in the [[Mythological Ritual]].
## Mechanics
Tracking increases the chance for [[Elusive]] mobs (e.g. [[Scatha]], [[Diamond Goblin]], [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] and [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Elusive|Sea Creatures]], [[Elusive|etc]].) to spawn.
The chance is calculated as follows:
FinalChance=BaseChance×(1+Tracking100)
For mobs that have a weight-based spawn chance, such as [[Sea Creatures]], the multiplier applies to their weight rather than their chance.

# Treasure Chance
Categories: Player Statistics, Fishing, Mechanics
[Treasure Chance]
Type: Stat
Uses: Determines the likelihood of a obtaining treasure whilst fishing with a Fishing Rod ([[Fishing Rods]])
Increasing: Fishing with [[Seal Ring]], with certain [[Fishing Rods]], Fishing Baits ([[Bait]]), and [[Pets]]
[Values]
Base Value: 0
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E025
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Chest ()
Treasure Chance is one of the player's [[Stats|Fishing Stats]]. It determines the likelihood of obtaining [[Treasure]] whilst [[Fishing]].
## Mechanics
Treasure Chance determines the chance a player has to catch [[Treasure]] while [[Fishing]], assuming they do not catch a [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]].
## Increasing Base Treasure Chance
Players have a base 0 Treasure Chance by default. It can be increased by:
- Leveling the [[Fishing]] Skill. (+0.1 per level), up to +5 Treasure Chance at [[Fishing|Fishing L (50)]]
- [[Ship|Pearl of the Swamp]]: +1 Treasure Chance

#### Enchantments
Certain [[Enchantments]] can be applied to items via an [[Enchantment Table]] or [[Anvil]] to grant Treasure Chance. The items with the [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] applied must be used to take effect.

Totaling 41.85 without accounting for sources unaffected by [[Bobbin' Time]]. Totaling 47.85 without accounting for [[Bobbin' Time]]. Totaling 49.9425 outside the [[Year of the Seal]]. Totaling 51.1425 .
## Tips
- [[Speed]], [[Attack Speed]], [[Ferocity]], [[Swing Range]], [[Mining Spread]], [[Gemstone Spread]], [[Timber]], [[Fishing Speed]], [[Trophy Chance]], Treasure Chance, [[Magic Find]] and [[Charm Chance]] are the only stats with a cap.
- Using either a [[Treasure Hook]] or 2+ pieces of [[Bronze Hunter Armor]] or higher tier can help with fishing treasure as they disable catching [[Sea Creatures]], which are rolled before treasure.

# Trophy Chance
Aliases: Trophy Fish Chance
Categories: Player Statistics, Fishing
[Trophy Chance]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the player's chance to catch [[Trophy Fish]] and [[Trophy Frogs]]
Increasing: [[Fishing Rods]], [[Armor]], [[Accessories]], [[Pets]], Baits ([[Bait]]), [[Attributes]]
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: 150
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E024
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Slugfish ()
Trophy Chance is one of the player's [[Stats|Fishing Stats]]. It increases the player's chance to catch [[Trophy (disambiguation)|Trophies]], such as [[Trophy Frogs]] and [[Trophy Fish]].
## Mechanics
Trophy Chance acts as a multiplier to the player's chance to catch each individual [[Trophy (disambiguation)|Trophy]] (when their catch conditions are met). It follows the formula:
FinalChance=BaseChance×(1+TrophyChance100)
For example, [[Trophy Fish|Lavahorse]] has a 4% base chance, which is multiplied by 1.5 for a player with 50 Trophy Chance, resulting in a final lavahorse chance of 6%.
Trophy Chance has a cap of 150 Trophy Chance, which is a 2.5x multiplier to the base chance for each individual trophy.
For further details on how trophies and their tiers are determined, see [[Trophy Fish]] and [[Trophy Frogs]].

- [[Ship|Pearl of the Lotus]]: +2.5 Trophy Chance

## Maximizing Trophy Chance
### Boostable Sources
The Trophy Chance from these sources can be raised with [[Bobbin' Time|Bobbin' Time V]] (+20% ).
- A 10✪ [[Hellfire Rod]] with a [[Trophy Line]] (+35)
- Wearing 3/4 [[Diamond Hunter Armor]] (+25)
  - While wearing 4/4 pieces gives higher base Trophy Chance, the bonus from [[Diamond Froggles]] makes the item (conditionally) best-in-slot.
- [[Geometric Oddity|Geometric]] [[Diamond Froggles]] that are [[Recombobulator 3000|recombobulated]] (+4)
- [[Prismarine Necklace]] (+1)
- [[Tropical Cloak]] (+1)
- [[Sponge Belt]] (+1)
- [[Clay Bracelet]] (+1)
- [[Odger's Tooth|Odger's Diamond Tooth]] (+4)
- [[PhD's Grimoire]] (+4)
- Having level 100 [[Spinosaurus Pet]] equipped with [[Hephaestus Relic]] during [[Weather]] (+11.25)
- Having level 100 [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Frog Pet]] equipped with [[Hephaestus Relic]] (+7.5)
- Having level 100 [[Rarity|EPIC]] or [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Kuudra Pet]] in pet menu (+20) (only while on the [[Crimson Isle]])
- Having level 10 in the [[Attributes|Trophy Hunter]] attribute (from [[Fire Eel Shard]] ) (+20)
- Having level 10 in the [[Attributes|Lotus Trophy]] attribute (from [[Lotusfish Shard]] ) (+10) (only while on the [[Lotus Atoll]])
- Being within the 30-block radius of a deployed [[Umberella]] during [[Weather]] (+10)
- Being on the [[Lotus Atoll]] during [[Weather|Blossoming]] (+5)
  - [[Blizzard in a Bottle Mixin]] (+0.5)
### Non-Boostable Sources
These are sources that won't increase Trophy Chance with the use of [[Bobbin' Time|Bobbin' Time V]], as their bonuses are not added to the player's stats directly.
- [[Hot Bait]] (+5)
- Fishing in a Trophy Chance Hotspot (+5)
  - This bonus can be raised to (+10) (doubled) with a [[Hotspot Sinker]]
- [[Diamond Froggles]]' bonus from fishing in a [[Wormhole]] (+7.5)
The maximum theoretically achievable Trophy Chance is 176.7 , though the stat has a cap of 150 .
## Trivia
- [[Speed]], [[Attack Speed]], [[Ferocity]], [[Swing Range]], [[Mining Spread]], [[Gemstone Spread]], [[Timber]], [[Fishing Speed]], Trophy Chance, [[Treasure Chance]], [[Magic Find]] and [[Charm Chance]] are the only stats with a cap.
- The effects of Trophy Chance can be nullified using [[Treasure Hook]] and [[Tera Shell Necklace]]. The former of which also negates the effects of [[Sea Creature Chance]] and [[Double Hook Chance]]. Interestingly, neither of the two items actually change the stat in any way.
  - Similarly, [[Lava Shell Necklace]] sets the player's [[Mending]] and [[Health Regen]] to 0, without disabling them.
  - [[Bonus Pest Chance]] can be disabled by the player using [[Pesthunter Badge]] and its upgrades.

# True Damage
Categories: Player Statistics, Mechanics
[True Damage]
Type: Stat
Uses: Determines damage dealt, ignoring [[Defense]]
Increasing: None by Player
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Symbol: ✷
Unicode Code Point: U+2738
True Damage behaves almost identically to regular [[Damage]], except it is unaffected by [[Defense]] or any status effects (such as [[Fire Resistance Potion|Fire Resistance]]). Upon taking True Damage, the player's [[Defense]] is momentarily set to their [[True Defense]]. True Damage is a vanilla Minecraft mechanic which is expanded upon in SkyBlock. It is similar to Magic Damage in vanilla Minecraft. The only way to decrease [[Damage]] from it is [[True Defense]], though some choose to use massive amounts of [[Health]] to combat it instead.
Very few things in the game utilize True Damage, with the most well known ones being the following:
- [[Trials of Fire|Trial of Fire]]
- [[Ender Dragon]]
- [[Ghost]]
- [[Skeleton Master]]
- [[Thorn|Thorn Minions]]
- [[Livid|Livid's Potion]]
- [[Storm|Storm's Giga Lightning]]
- [[Sven Packmaster]]
- [[Inferno Demonlord]]
There is currently only one known way for a player to deal True Damage: through the [[Spider Queen's Stinger]], an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Bows|Bow]].
## Trivia
- True Damage is generally used to prevent players from using very tanky gear against mobs and tank their attacks with ease. Therefore, strong bosses often deal high amounts of True Damage. The strongest bosses of different [[Combat]] aspects either deal comparably high [[Damage]], or a lot of True Damage.
  - [[Mastiff Armor]] or [[Sorrow Armor]] can be viable against these mobs, however neither of these are dungeonizable, making them unviable against most sources of it in dungeons.
- Master Mode Storm deals 999,999,999 True Damage during his Giga Lightning.

|Oct 11, 2019|0.7.2|Added True Damage.|

# True Defense
Categories: Player Statistics, Confirmations needed, Mechanics, Defense, True Defense
[True Defense]
Type: Stat
Uses: Reduces [[True Damage]]
Increasing: [[True Protection]], [[Reflection]], [[Sorrow Armor]], [[True Resistance Potion]]
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E027
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Bone Meal ()
True Defense is one of the player's [[Stats|combat stats]]. It reduces [[True Damage]], however True Defense does not reduce regular [[Damage]].
## Mechanics
### Damage Reduction
The amount of [[True Damage]] a player takes is reduced depending on the player's True Defense. It is calculated with this formula: Damage Reduction=True Defense100+True Defense Normal [[Damage]] is not affected by True Defense.

 result    = inner

- [[Candidate Jerry]] - +10%

- Weapon - +150

  - [[Chimera|Chimera V]]: Copies +100% of your active Pet's stats. - +75
- Armor - +344 & +32%
  - 3/4 [[Sorrow Armor]] [ [[Recombobulator 3000]] + [[Dragon Horn]] + [[Gemstone|Perfect Opal Gemstone]] + [[Legion|Legion V]] with 20 Players + [[Small Brain|Small Brain V]]] - +204 & +24%

- [[SOS Flare]] with a [[Power Scrolls|Opal Power Scroll]] applied
  - SOS Flare when shot - +30
  - When using the SOS Flare's RIGHT CLICK ability, the Opal Power Scroll take effect for 5s. - +10
- [[Blazetekk™ Ham Radio]] - +40 when the player is on the same channel within 110 blocks with one and only one player. [Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- [[Potions|Wisp's Ice-Flavored Water I]] - +25
- [[Equipment]] - +49.5
  - Necklace: Any [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Necklaces|Necklace]] [ [[Recombobulator 3000]] + [[Shiny Prism]] + [[Cayenne|Cayenne V]]] - +5
  - One of the following [[Cloaks]] - +15
    - [[David's Cloak]] [with [[Attributes|Cloak Improvement]] 10 + 150 [[Attributes]] + [[Shiny Prism]] + [[Cayenne|Cayenne V]]]
    - [[Golden Fleece]] [ [[Recombobulator 3000]] + [[Shiny Prism]] + [[Cayenne|Cayenne V]]]
  - Belt: Any [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Belts|Belt]] [ [[Recombobulator 3000]] + [[Shiny Prism]] + [[Cayenne|Cayenne V]]] - +5
  - Glove/Bracelet : [[Demonslayer Gauntlet]] [ [[Recombobulator 3000]] + [[Shiny Prism]] + [[Cayenne|Cayenne V]]] - +24.5
- [[Accessories]] - +120.6

- [[Heart of the Forest]] with Galatea's Might while on [[Galatea]] - +25%
- [[Attributes|Veil]] 10 - +10

# Wisdom
Aliases: Global Wisdom
Categories: Player Statistics
[Wisdom]
Stat Category
Wisdom is a type of [[Stats|Stat]] that increases the [[Skills|Skill]] XP the player gains for a specific skill. There are different Wisdom Stats for each skill.
|Skill|Wisdom|
|-|-|
|[[Combat]]|[[Combat Wisdom]]|
|[[Farming]]|[[Farming Wisdom]]|
|[[Fishing]]|[[Fishing Wisdom]]|
|[[Mining]]|[[Mining Wisdom]]|
|[[Foraging]]|[[Foraging Wisdom]]|
|[[Enchanting]]|[[Enchanting Wisdom]]|
|[[Alchemy]]|[[Alchemy Wisdom]]|
|[[Carpentry]]|[[Carpentry Wisdom]]|
|[[Runecrafting]]|[[Runecrafting Wisdom]]|
|[[Taming]]|[[Taming Wisdom]]|
|[[Social]]|[[Social Wisdom]]|
|[[Hunting]]|[[Hunting Wisdom]]|
## Mechanics
Wisdom for each Skill increases the amount of Skill XP gained for that Skill. When gaining Skill XP, the amount gained is multiplied by 1+Wisdom100. For example, if the player gains +10 [[Combat|Combat XP]] and has 10 [[Combat Wisdom]], they will gain +10% of that XP, or +11 [[Combat|Combat XP]].

# Brews
Aliases: Bitter Ice Tea, Brew
Categories: Potions, Brewing, Brews
[Brews]
Brews are [[Potions|Potion]] modifiers that can be used in place of [[Awkward Potion|Awkward Potions]] to add additional stat bonuses and abilities to the [[Potions|item]].
## Brews
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Effect|Source|Affected Potions|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|

## Trivia
- All Brews have the tag "Basic Brew", despite there being no non-basic ones.
- Drinking a potion made with a brew will give the player a [[Glass Bottle]], despite it not being part of the brewing recipe.
- Players can gain the effect of the same brew via multiple potions.

# Deployable Skins
Aliases: Deployable Skin, Power Orb Skin, Power Orb Skins
Categories: Power Orbs, Cosmetics
[Deployable Skins]
The Glistening Melon Power Orb Skin, which can be applied to [[Power Orbs]] to change their appearance.
Deployable Skins are cosmetic items that are able to be applied to [[Deployables]] to change their appearance. They can be purchased from [[Fire Sale|Fire Sales]] for [[SkyBlock Gems]].
A Power Orb Skin can be added on a [[Power Orbs|Power Orb]] by combining the desired skin with a [[Power Orbs|Power Orb]] in an [[Anvil]].

# NPC
Aliases: Characters, NPCs
Categories: Quest giver, Items, Non-player characters, Quests
[NPC]
NPCs, or Non-Player Characters, are characters that are found scattered around [[SkyBlock]]. Players can interact with NPCs to perform various actions.
## Interactions
NPCs can be typically be interacted with by right-clicking them. Most of them have dialogue that is triggered, often times multiple lines of dialogue under different conditions. These dialogues may provide information that help players to navigate in the game and interact with different game mechanics.
Some NPCs lead the player to starting or proceeding with a [[Quests|Quest]].
Some NPCs feature [[Shops]] with various items items. Most of these shops have a daily buy limit of 640 items, which resets at 12:00 AM GMT, or 8:00 PM EST. For NPCs that do sell items, it is possible to profit by buying these items and selling them for higher than their purchase price on the [[Bazaar]]. This technique is known as [[Bazaar|NPC Flipping]].

# Romero and Juliette
Aliases: Romeo and Juliet
Categories: Quests
Romero and Juliette is a [[Quests|Quest]] in [[The Park]], telling the story of the love between [[Romero]] and [[Juliette]].
## Quest Summary
To start the quest, you must speak with Juliette (Romero won't appear otherwise). The rest of the quest requires you to find Romero in separate locations and fetching items for him, which you will give to Juliette each time. Be warned that some of the required items are expensive and should only be finished in the late or end game.
As of v0.19.5 update, the quest requires at least [[Combat|Combat XXII (22)]] to complete.
For further details on each step, have a look below the table.
Each step of the quest gives an upgraded form of the [[Ring of Love]], which gives a percentage chance to deal +100% [[Damage]]. For brevity, the table will only list the odds.
Note: Required items will be consumed, except for the Tuxedo and any gear used to reach enough [[Intelligence]] for the 7th step.
|Step|Romero location|Required item (Romero)|Required item for Juliette (Romero reward)|Reward|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|1|Cave at the [[Savanna Woodland]]; -456, 100, 30 ([[Coordinates]])|1 Yellow Rock ([[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]])|Poorly Wrapped Rock ([[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]])|Shiny Yellow Rock ([[Ring of Love]]); COMMON ([[Rarity]]); Odds: 1 in 1 trillion|
|2|[[Village]], near [[Marco]]'s Hut; 89, 75, 15 ([[Coordinates]])|15 Poppy ([[Flowers]])|Rose Bouquet ([[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]])|Yellow Rock of Love ([[Ring of Love]]); COMMON ([[Rarity]]); Odds: 1 in 100 billion|
|3|[[Graveyard]] at the Emerald Altar; -118, 74, -171 ([[Coordinates]])|64 (1 stack) [[Emerald]]|Fake Emerald Altar ([[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]])|Mediocre Ring of Love ([[Ring of Love]]); UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]); Odds: 1 in 10 billion|
|4|Near the entrance of the [[Crimson Isle]]; -389, 94, -479 ([[Coordinates]])|1 [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]]|Mushroom & Warts Stew ([[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]])|Rubbish Ring of Love ([[Ring of Love]]); UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]); Odds: 1 in 1 billion|
|5|[[Mountain]], wood platform to the [[Ruins]] side; -54, 169, 73 ([[Coordinates]])|[[Potions]] 1x Rabbit VI Potion ([[Rabbit Potion]]) with [[Cheap Coffee]], [[Decent Coffee]] or [[Black Coffee]] base|Chunk of the Moon ([[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]])|Modest Ring of Love ([[Ring of Love]]); RARE ([[Rarity]]); Odds: 1 in 100 million|
|6|[[Gold Mine]], down next to the campfire; -22, 35, -339 ([[Coordinates]])|1 [[Enchanted Lava Bucket]]|Secret Gift for Juliette ([[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]])|Refined Ring of Love ([[Ring of Love]]); RARE ([[Rarity]]); Odds: 1 in 1 million|
|7|[[Wilderness]] – treehouse with a red and white roof, near [[Tia the Fairy]]; 145, 112, 114 ([[Coordinates]])|1,291 [[Intelligence]] NOTE: This equals 1,391 Max Mana ([[Mana]]).|Solved Rubix Prism ([[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]])|Classy Ring of Love ([[Ring of Love]]); RARE ([[Rarity]]); Odds: 1 in 100 thousand|
|8|Right side of the [[Colosseum]], near the small pool; 130, 68, 37 ([[Coordinates]])|1 [[Emerald Blade]] NOTE: An Emerald Blade that has been donated to the museum will work.|Poem of True Love ([[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]])|Exquisite Ring of Love ([[Ring of Love]]); EPIC ([[Rarity]]); Odds: 1 in 10 thousand|
|9|[[Mushroom Desert]], in a small house; 297, 87, -562 ([[Coordinates]])|1 [[Flower Minion]]|Flower Maelstrom ([[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]])|Invaluable Ring of Love ([[Ring of Love]]); EPIC ([[Rarity]]); Odds: 1 in 1 thousand|
|10|Right by Juliette|[[Tuxedo]] from [[Seymour]] (the cheap one is good enough and costs 3M [[Coins]] – unlike other items you keep the tuxedo afterwards)|Very Official Yellow Rock of Love! ([[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]])|Legendary Ring of Love ([[Ring of Love]]); LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]); Odds: 1 in 100|
## Further Details
Start the quest by speaking with Juliette.
Gold Mine
At Quest Step 1, Romero can first be found behind and below [[Master Tactician Funk]] in a cave at the [[Savanna Woodland]]. Upon interacting with him, he asks you to get him 1 [[Romero and Juliette Quest Items|Yellow Rock]], which can be found by mining the gold ore in one of the savanna caves (do not use a [[Silk Touch]] pickaxe). Once obtained, he will pass you a [[Romero and Juliette Quest Items|Poorly Wrapped Rock]], which you must then pass to Juliette.
At Quest Step 2, Romero can be found at the [[Village]], near [[Marco]]'s hut. He then asks you to obtain 16 [[Flowers|Poppy]] for him. Upon obtaining them, interacting with Romero will give you a [[Romero and Juliette Quest Items|Rose Bouquet]], which you also need to pass to Juliette.
The Emerald Altar
At Quest Step 3, Romero can be found at the Emerald Altar in the [[Graveyard]], to the left. He then needs you to pass him 64 [[Emerald]]. Once you pass them to him, he will return a [[Romero and Juliette Quest Items|Fake Emerald Altar]] which you questionably pass to Juliette.
At Quest Step 4, Romero can be found in the [[Crimson Isle]], in one of the underground [[Nether Wart]] farms. He will require an [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]], which consists of 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Mushroom|Red Mushroom]].
At Quest Step 5, Romero is found at the wooden platform on top of the Mountain. He complains about the cold and asks for a Rabbit VI potion (Splash [[Potions]] does not work) brewed with Coffee, purchased from the [[Bartender]]. To brew the potion, you need an [[Enchanted Rabbit Foot]] and an [[Enchanted Glowstone]] (Or an [[Enchanted Redstone Lamp]]).
At Quest Step 6, Romero is found in the [[Gold Mine]] next to a campfire. He is straight down and steep to the left. He will ask for an [[Enchanted Lava Bucket]].
Romero's treehouse
At Quest Step 7, Romero is found at the house with a red and white roof in the [[Wilderness]], close to the [[Dark Auction]]. He asks for at least 1,291 [[Intelligence]]. Note that this means a max [[Mana]] of 1,391.
Items to be considered:
- [[Aurora Armor]], which has quite high [[Intelligence]] stat for its price.
- [[Ender Monocle]], extremely cheap power stone providing great [[Intelligence]].
- [[Crystal Armor]] or [[Bat Person Armor]] which can have doubled or tripled stats under certain conditions. This can be combined with the [[Reforging|Wise]] or [[Reforging|Necrotic]] reforges (the latter gives more [[Intelligence]]).
  - Crystal Armour can receive up to a 15 (187.5%) bonus by jumping next to the torch inside of the hut.
- [[Bottle of Jyrre]]
- Held items that give [[Intelligence]], such as [[Dreadlord Sword]], [[Bonzo's Staff]], or the more expensive [[Spirit Sceptre]], reforged to [[Reforging|Heroic]].
At Quest Step 8, Romero appears on the right side of the [[Colosseum]] beside the smaller pool of water to the right of the entrance, where he asks for an [[Emerald Blade]].
The location and appearance of Romero's desert hut
At Quest Step 9, Romero appears in a small house in the [[Mushroom Desert]] at [[Coordinates|297, 87, -562]]. He asks for a [[Flower Minion]] from the [[Dark Auction]]. This is often the most expensive part of the quest, due to the high demand for Flower Minions.
At Quest Step 10, you need to get a tuxedo from [[Taylor]]'s basement, costing 3 million to 75 million coins. Note that the tuxedo is not consumed by the quest. At this point, your [[Rarity|EPIC]] talisman will become [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]]. You also unlock the "Friar Lawrence" [[Achievements|Achievement]].
Completing this whole quest can cost approximately 50,000,000 [[Coins]] or more. It can vary depending on the price of a Flower Minion.
## Gallery
- How the Romero's treehouse used to look like before update 0.24.2.
## Trivia
- The scrambled Rubix Prism Romero is holding during Quest Step 7 cannot be solved, as it contains duplicate pieces and a piece with two orange-colored sides at once.

# Chicken Race
Categories: Race, Events, Season of Jerry, V0.7.5
The Chicken Race starting point.
The Chicken Race is a race located in [[Jerry's Workshop]] next to [[Gulliver]] at [[Coordinates|68, 105, 33]], the player controls a [[Chicken]] that jumps through hoops in the sky. The race will end if the player touches the ground, or leaves the chicken (such as [[Aspect of the End]], or pressing the shift key), or the timer ends. Passing through cluckpoints increases the time on the timer, and reaching a cluckpoint for the first time will end the race and give the player a reward.
The chicken race won't progress past cluckpoint 1 if the player hasn't talked to [[Frosty]] yet.
## Rewards
|Cluckpoint|Time Required|Icon|Reward|Rarity|Description|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|1|<33s|[[Red Gift]]|[[Red Gift]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Lets the user and another player both claim a reward.|
|2|<52s|[[Gift Compass]]|[[Gift Compass]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|When held, points to the nearest [[White Gift]] that hasn't been found this year.|
|3|<68s|[[Frozen Chicken]]|[[Frozen Chicken]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|+1 [[Speed]]. Also grants 8 [[Accessory Power]] in the [[Accessory Bag]] as a RARE ([[Rarity]]) Accessory ([[Accessories]]).|
|4|<104s|[[Jingle Bells]]|[[Jingle Bells]]; +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Angers all monsters in a 10 block range, consuming 50% of your total [[Mana]] and causing them to run towards you.|
## Tips
- Try to cut corners as much as possible.
- Try to land on the edge of the ring, as it maximizes time.
- Try not to over- or underestimate the Y-axis distance on the next ring, as either falling or jumping with no change in the X and Z-axis will cost you time.
- Don't worry if the game lags, it's normal for an event, so don't be surprised if you have some freezes or if you have to pass through a ring multiple times.
- Be careful not to press the space bar too much during the race, your chicken will jump randomly so you miss the rings.
- The chicken moves forward automatically, you don't need to press W. Doing so may make you go slower.
- When next to the lake, be careful as the fishing rods may kill the chicken.
- Save as much time as possible, especially if doing the 4th cluckpoint, as you will fail with less than 1 second left if you make a simple mistake.
## Trivia
- The chickens that players ride on are able to be killed which results in the player on the chicken getting reset.

# Dungeon Hub Race
Aliases: Dungeon Race, Dungeon race
Categories: Race
The Dungeon Hub Races are three races that take place in the [[Dungeon Hub]], and all are started at [[Guildford]]. The three races are the Crystal Core Race, the Giant Mushroom Race, and the Precursor Ruins Race. Each race has 4 settings, each with their own restrictions. There is also the option to enable Runback, which turns the endpoint into a checkpoint and requires the player to go back to the start in order to finish the race.
The coordinates of the starting point and the three destinations are as follows:
Starting Point: [[Coordinates|-19, 86, 0]]
Crystal Core: [[Coordinates|5, 11, -3]]
Giant Mushrooms: [[Coordinates|-1, 23, 101]]
Precursor Ruins: [[Coordinates|-137, 21, -3]]
## Race Settings
Anything: Abilities, [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearls]] and [[Grappling Hook|Grappling Hooks]] are allowed.
No Pearls: Ender Pearls are disabled. Abilities and Grappling Hooks are allowed.
No Abilities: Abilities and Ender Pearls are disabled. Grappling Hooks are allowed.
Nothing at all: Abilities, Ender Pearls and Grappling Hooks are disabled. [[Speed|Walk speed]] is capped at 130%.
For each race setting, there are 4 available time trials (and another 4 when Runback is enabled). These times vary for each race.
## Rewards
Completing each time trial rewards the player with [[Wither Essence]] unless they qualify for a Talisman. Level 1 gives 3, level 2 gives 4, level 3 gives 5 and level 4 gives 6. In total (1 way & runback), you can get up to 432x Wither Essence just by racing. (18 wither essence per setting, 4 settings per race, 3 races and plus runback. So 18 x 4 x 3 x 2 = 432)
The reward for completing 4/4 for the same race on a particular setting is the [[Cat Talisman]].
Completing 4/4 for another race rewards the player with the Cat Talisman's upgraded form, the [[Lynx Talisman]].
Completing 4/4 for a third race will reward the player with the Lynx Talisman's upgraded form, the [[Cheetah Talisman]].
## Tips and Strategies
When the player reaches the end of a one way race, it teleports them back to the start, creating an exploit regarding the Anything mode. Just right before being teleported back, a player can throw a [[Ender Pearl]] up then quickly start another race. The ender pearl teleports them to the end pad after landing, allowing for the player to finish the race in mere seconds.
For "Nothing at all" races, despite the walking speed being limited to 130%, jump height is not affected. This means these races can be done with the jump boost given by the [[Rabbit Potion]]. This becomes useful when coming across with ladders and vines (e.g. Climbing back up the cliff when returning to the start point for Precursor Ruins race), that can be skipped by boosted jumps.
(WIP How to complete Crystal Core nothing runback Goal 4?)

# End Race
Aliases: End race, The End Race
Categories: Race
The End Race is a [[Island Racing|race]] located in [[The End]], which can be started by talking with [[Guber]] at [[Coordinates|-495, 121, -242]].
The goal of the end race is to reach the far end of the End Island and back in the shortest time frame possible. All types of pearls are blocked during the race and cannot be used.
It is suggested to have a high [[Intelligence]] count, in order to have enough mana to use many movement items.
## Rewards
For completing The Race in:
|Time|Reward|
|-|-|
|3 minutes or less|8 Silent Pearls ([[Silent Pearl]])|
|2 minutes or less|[[Enderman Mask]]|
|80 seconds or less|[[Nope the Fish]]|
|48 seconds or less|[[Pig's Foot]]|
|42 seconds or less|The End Race ([[Achievements]]) Achievement|
|Any amount of time with Blindness ([[Blindness Potion]]) active|[[Obscure Ending]]|
## Coordinates
|Location|Coordinates|
|-|-|
|Start|-502, 121, -241 ([[Coordinates]])|
|Checkpoint|-774, 101, -299 ([[Coordinates]])|
|End|-502, 121, -241 ([[Coordinates]])|
## Tips
To complete the end race, players can:
- Use a [[Grappling Hook]]. Players can complete the race in 48 seconds with only a [[Grappling Hook]].
- Use an [[Aspect of the End]] and a [[Grappling Hook]] to fly. A [[Leaping Sword]] may help too.
- Use [[Bat Person Armor]] and a [[Grappling Hook]] to fly.
- [[Grappling Hook]] and [[Tarantula Boots]]/ [[Spider's Boots|Spider Boots]] combo to fly.
- In terms of Speed, the [[Young Dragon Armor]] is the best choice, as it allows to complete the first three tiers easily.
- Beware of the cliffs leading to the [[Dragon's Nest]] at the middle part of the race, if you fall in them, the race will be canceled and you will most likely die or take heavy fall damage.
- You can also take a [[Remnant of the Eye]] and jump off the island after completing the first checkpoint to instantly teleport back to spawn where you can simply climb some stairs back up to the finish point.

# Rift Race
Categories: Race
The Rift Race is a race located in the [[West Village]]. The player must run over 10 checkpoints, marked on the ground as gold pressure plates in the time frame given.
## Rewards
|Race|Icon|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|60s|[[Dark Pebble]]|[[Dark Pebble]]|
|35s|[[Gunthesizer Lichen]]|[[Gunthesizer Lichen]]|
## Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Starting the race|RIFT RACING Race started! Good luck!|
|Restarting the race timer|RIFT RACING Timer reset to 0:00!|
|Canceling the race|RIFT RACING Race cancelled!|
|Reaching a checkpoint|You crossed checkpoint # out of 10!|
|Completing the race|RIFT RACING Race finished in ##:##.###!|
## Trivia
- Canceling the Rift Race fully replenishes the player's [[Mana]].
## Tips
- Try to cut corners as much as possible.
- Use an [[Aspect of the Leech]] to easily get across gaps that would be hard to jump across.
- Getting within 1 block of a checkpoint counts as touching it.

# Abicases
Aliases: Abicase, Abiphone Case, Abiphone case, Actually Blue Abicase, Actually Blue™ Abicase, Blue but Green Abicase, Blue™ but Green Abicase, Blue™ but Red Abicase, Blue™ but Yellow Abicase, Lighter Blue™ Abicase, Rezar Abicase, Rezar® Abicase, Sumsung 3G Abicase, Sumsung GG Abicase, Sumsung© G3 Abicase, Sumsung© GG Abicase, Sumsung™ 3G Abicase, Sumsung™ GG Abicase
Categories: Rare items, Items, Bits Shop
[Abicases]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Bits Shop]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Shop]
Merchants: [[Elizabeth]]
Buy: 15,000–26,000 [[Bits]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ABICASE_BLUE_BLUE; ABICASE_BLUE_GREEN; ABICASE_BLUE_RED; ABICASE_BLUE_YELLOW; ABICASE_BLUE_AQUA; ABICASE_REZAR; ABICASE_SUMSUNG_1; ABICASE_SUMSUNG_2
Tooltip: Adds +1 contact slot.; Grants +1 Magical Power per 2; contacts in your Abiphone.; Only ONE case will have an effect while in accessory bag.; RARE ACCESSORY
Abicases are [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Accessories]] that grant +1 Accessory Power for every 2 contacts the [[Player]] has in their [[Abiphones|Abiphone]], as well as giving special bonuses.
## Abicases
Sumsung focuses on the technology. Upgrade your Abiphone remotely through cloud-based blockchain agile immutable dev-ops fuzzy cases.
|Slot|Name|Bonus|Cost|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Abicases]]|Sumsung© G3 Abicase|Adds +1 contact slot.|15,000 [[Bits]]|
|[[Abicases]]|Sumsung© GG Abicase|Adds +2 contact slots.|25,000 [[Bits]]|
Play hard, play fair and do it in all green. Perfect for those who have time to grind but no time to call their close ones.
|Slot|Name|Bonus|Cost|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Abicases]]|Rezar® Abicase|Reduces Abiphone calling to a single ringing.|26,000 [[Bits]]|
Blue Abicases are not all blue. Their color reflects your personality, your life and your legacy. Think of it this way: Is your personality just a recolor of last year's? Blue™ says... well maybe!
|Slot|Name|Bonus|Cost|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Abicases]]|Blue™ but Red Abicase|+1.5 [[Combat Wisdom]]|17,000 [[Bits]]|
|[[Abicases]]|Actually Blue™ Abicase|+1.5 [[Mining Wisdom]]|17,000 [[Bits]]|
|[[Abicases]]|Blue™ but Green Abicase|+1.5 [[Foraging Wisdom]]|17,000 [[Bits]]|
|[[Abicases]]|Blue™ but Yellow Abicase|+1.5 [[Farming Wisdom]]|17,000 [[Bits]]|
|[[Abicases]]|Lighter Blue™ Abicase|+1.5 [[Fishing Wisdom]]|17,000 [[Bits]]|
## Trivia
- Abicase company names are puns on real-life company names.
  - 'Sumsung' is a parody of the company Samsung.
  - 'Rezar' is a parody of Razer Inc., a company that produces PC and gaming products.
  - 'Blue' is a parody of (RED), a company which partners with other companies to design special products, notably including the special edition (PRODUCT)RED iPhones.
- As of the 0.24.3 update, players can store 80 unique contacts in their [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] giving additional 40 Accessory Power on top of what the accessory gives (+12 at [[Rarity|EPIC]] rarity), making it the best accessory for Accessory Power with 8 more than [[Hegemony Artifact]]
- As described in the item tooltip, only one abicase can apply its effect while stored in the accessory bag. Because of how these effects are applied, you can gain the bonuses of two abicases simultaneously by placing a Rezar® or any Sumsung© abicase in your accessory bag while keeping any Blue™ abicase in your main inventory (since the wisdom bonus from a Blue™ abicase does not rely on an effect). Inherently, this setup will not work if the items are placed in the reverse locations.

### Fishing
An Abyssal Lanternfish Shard can be obtained from fishing in the [[Dwarven Mines]] with [[Wooden Bait]]. There is a 100% (1 in 1 rolls) chance for [[Treasure|GOOD CATCH]] drops to be replaced with one.
### Huntraps
An Abyssal Lanternfish Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Dwarven Mines]] (Underwater).
### Fusion
An Abyssal Lanternfish Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].

[Shop]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: STARLYN_PRIZE
Tooltip: Used to purchase certain items from Agatha's Shop!; RARE
Agatha's Prize is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item.
## Obtaining
Agatha's Prize has a 10% chance of being awarded from an [[Rarity|EPIC]] or a 20% chance from a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] bracket in a [[Starlyn Contest]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Usage
Agatha's Prizes are used to purchase various [[Foraging]] related items or bonuses from [[Agatha]].
### NPC Trading
|Name|Requirements|Ingredients|
|-|-|-|
|[[Fig Fortune]] 41 - 50|Agatha's Milestone I ([[Agatha]])|[[Agatha's Prize]] 1x Agatha's Prize per level|
|[[Mangrove Fortune]] 41 - 50|Agatha's Milestone I ([[Agatha]])|[[Agatha's Prize]] 1x Agatha's Prize per level|
|First [[Foraging]] Level Cap|Agatha's Milestone IV ([[Agatha]])|[[Agatha's Prize]] 2x Agatha's Prize|
|Second [[Foraging]] Level Cap|Agatha's Milestone IV ([[Agatha]])|[[Agatha's Prize]] 5x Agatha's Prize|
|[[Shiniest]]|Agatha's Milestone V ([[Agatha]])|[[Agatha's Prize]] 20x Agatha's Prize|
## Trivia
- To obtain the 47 Agatha's Prizes needed to purchase all upgrades and a [[Shiniest]], it would take on average 78 hours with [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] brackets or above.

The Alert Flare is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Deployables|Deployable]]. It is unlocked at [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer VI (6)]] and can be upgraded into an [[SOS Flare]]. Healing effect only working on [[Crimson Isle]] and [[Kuudra]].

### Crafting

Deployable:
Alert Flare
[Effects]
[Ability: Deploy RIGHT CLICK; Shoot the flare up in the sky for 3m buffing up to 8 players within 40 blocks.; Vitality Cost: 10 Vitality; Mana Cost: 300 Mana; Cooldown: 20s; Flare Buff: Alert Flare; Grants +50% base mana regen.; Gain +15 Vitality.; Gain +20 True Defense.; Heal 40 Health per second while on the Crimson Isle and Kuudra.; Gain +10 Ferocity.; Only one deployable buff applies.]

The Amber-Polished Drill Engine is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Drill Parts|Drill Part]] that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. It increases [[Drills]]' [[Mining Speed]] by +600 and [[Mining Fortune]] by +100.
## Obtaining
The Amber-Polished Drill Engine can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain IX (9)]]. It takes 30 seconds to forge.

## Usage
The Amber-Polished Drill Engine can be applied to any [[Drills|Drill]] via [[Drill Anvil]], to increase its stats by +600 [[Mining Speed]] and +100 [[Mining Fortune]]. It can then be removed via the same menu for 9,999 [[Coins]], which gives the item back to the player.

An Ananke Feather is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item.
## Obtaining
### Kuudra
An Ananke Feather has a 0.27% (1 in 369.95 runs) chance of being obtained from an [[Kuudra|Infernal Kuudra]] run's paid reward chest.
The chance of dropping an Ananke Feather can be increased by 2%–20% through levelling up the [[Attributes|Wings of Destiny]] by syphoning [[Ananke Shard|Ananke Shards]].
### Salvaging Godrolls
Ananke Feathers can also be obtained by salvaging legacy greyed out attributes from certain items with specific attribute combinations at [[Salvage Old Attributes]] or in /attributemenu.

# Ancestral Spade
Categories: Rare items, Items, Crafting ingredients
[Ancestral Spade]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Archaic Spade]]
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✕ No
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Diana]]
Buy: 5,000 [[Coins]] 32x [[Gold Ingot]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ANCESTRAL_SPADE
Tooltip: Use to reveal and dig up Griffin Burrows in the hub while Diana's Mythological Ritual is active.; Maximum Burrow Chain Length: 4; Burrows dug with this item may contain:; ● Monsters ● Griffin Feathers ● Coins; Ability: Echo RIGHT CLICK Show the way to the next or nearby Griffin burrow.; Cooldown: 3s; This item can be reforged!; RARE
The Ancestral Spade is a [[Rarity|RARE]] tool that can be bought from [[Diana]] when she is elected [[Mayor Election|Mayor]] or [[Mayor Election|Minister]]. It allows the player to find [[Griffin Burrows]] when used on the [[Hub Island]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
The Ancestral Spade can be bought from [[Diana]] in the [[Community Center]] for 5,000 [[Coins]] and 32 [[Gold Ingot|Gold Ingots]] when she is elected [[Mayor Election|Mayor]] or [[Mayor Election|Minister]], regardless of her active perks.
## Usage
Item:
Ancestral Spade
[Effects]
[Use to reveal and dig up Griffin Burrows in the Hub (Hub Island) while Diana Diana's Mythological Ritual is active.; Maximum Burrow Chain Length: 4 Ability: Echo RIGHT CLICK; Show the way to the next or nearby Griffin burrow.; Cooldown: 3s]
The [[Ancestral Spade]] Ancestral Spade's Echo right-click ability points the player towards the nearest [[Griffin Burrows|Griffin Burrow]] when used on the [[Hub Island]], and it can be also used to dig out burrows by left clicking them.
The available loot from treasure burrows is as follows, depending on the tier of the spade. It does not depend on the rarity of the player's [[Griffin Pet]].
|Slot|Item|Weight|Chance per Treasure|Chance per Burrow No Reforge|Chance per Burrow Erudite ([[Reforging]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Griffin Feather]]|[[Griffin Feather]]|64|66.67% (1 in 1.5 treasures)|12.50% (1 in 8 burrows)|13.89% (1 in 7.2 burrows)|
|[[Coins]] x2500|2,500 [[Coins]]|15|15.62% (1 in 6.4 treasures)|2.93% (1 in 34.13 burrows)|3.26% (1 in 30.72 burrows)|
|[[Coins]] x5000|5,000 [[Coins]]|10|10.42% (1 in 9.6 treasures)|1.95% (1 in 51.2 burrows)|2.17% (1 in 46.08 burrows)|
|[[Coins]] x10k|10,000 [[Coins]]|5|5.21% (1 in 19.2 treasures)|0.98% (1 in 102.4 burrows)|1.09% (1 in 92.16 burrows)|

### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Archaic Spade]]|None|[[Ancestral Spade]] 1x Ancestral Spade 1x [[Hilt of Revelations]] 2x [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 8x [[Enchanted Ancient Claw]] 32x [[Griffin Feather]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Ancient Claw]] x2 ×4 + [[Griffin Feather]] x32 + [[Enchanted Gold Block]] ×2 + Ancestral Spade + [[Hilt of Revelations]] → [[Archaic Spade]]|
## Tips
- Players who cannot see the particles generated by the Ancestral Spade's Echo ability may need to turn up their particle quality using the /particlequality command and ensure particles are enabled in their Minecraft client as well.
## Trivia
- The maximum length of a [[Griffin Burrows|Griffin Burrow]] chain achievable with the Ancestral Spade is 4, or 6 with the [[Reforging|Erudite]] reforge when [[Daedalus' Notes]] is applied to it.
- When using the Ancestral Spade's ability, there is a 1% chance to play a random game sound instead of the usual sound, likely as an Easter egg.

# Andesite Whetstone
Aliases: Refined, Refined Amber
Categories: Rare items, Reforge stones
[Andesite Whetstone]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Refined
Applied To: Mining Tools ([[Tools]])
Apply Cost: 100,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XXV (25) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
