[Shop]
Merchants: [[Smithmonger]]
Buy: 1,000,000 [[Coins]]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: REFINED_AMBER
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Refined reforge when combined with a Mining Tool.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:; ☯ Mining Wisdom; Refined Bonus You have a 0.25% chance to find enchanted forms of items when mining Ores.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 25.; RARE REFORGE STONE
Andesite Whetstone is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Refined]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]]. It grants [[Mining Wisdom]].
## Obtaining
Andesite Whetstone can be purchased from the [[Smithmonger]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Andesite Whetstone can be applied to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Refined]] reforge, to grant [[Mining Wisdom]], which scales with the Mining Tool's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXV (25)]]. A Mining Tool that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Refined]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Refined Bonus, which grants 0.25% (1 in 400) chance to obtain enchanted forms of items when mining [[Ores]].
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+1 Mng.W ([[Mining Wisdom]])|+2 Mng.W ([[Mining Wisdom]])|+3 Mng.W ([[Mining Wisdom]])|+4 Mng.W ([[Mining Wisdom]])|+6 Mng.W ([[Mining Wisdom]])|+8 Mng.W ([[Mining Wisdom]])|+10 Mng.W ([[Mining Wisdom]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Refined Bonus; You have a 0.25% chance to find enchanted forms of items when mining Ores.|Refined Bonus; You have a 0.25% chance to find enchanted forms of items when mining Ores.|Refined Bonus; You have a 0.25% chance to find enchanted forms of items when mining Ores.|Refined Bonus; You have a 0.25% chance to find enchanted forms of items when mining Ores.|Refined Bonus; You have a 0.25% chance to find enchanted forms of items when mining Ores.|Refined Bonus; You have a 0.25% chance to find enchanted forms of items when mining Ores.|Refined Bonus; You have a 0.25% chance to find enchanted forms of items when mining Ores.|
|Cost to apply|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- The drop will be the first enchanted item in the line of the ore, for example: [[Ores|Coal Ore]] gives [[Enchanted Coal]] instead of [[Enchanted Coal Block]].
- It was common for scammers to use this reforge on normal [[Mithril Pickaxe|Mithril Pickaxes]] in an attempt to make them pass as [[Refined Mithril Pickaxe]].
  - This also used to be the case with [[Titanium Pickaxe|Titanium Pickaxes]] until they got renamed to [[Polished Titanium Pickaxe|Polished Titanium Pickaxes]].
- Despite not being listed in the lore, the Refined Bonus also works on [[Dwarven Metals]]. However, unlike [[Compact]], it does not work on [[Blocks]], nor does it work on [[Gemstone|Gemstones]].

The Anteater Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Floor Fortune]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
An Anteater Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Queen Ant Shard]]
- Second: [[King Cobra Shard]]
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Anteater Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Floor Fortune]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Floor Fortune ([[Attributes]]); Grants a +0.1%–1% chance to gain double loot from [[Floor Drops]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

Categories: Rare items, Pet Skins, Fire Sales
[Aquamarine Rabbit Skin]
Type: Pet Skin ([[Pet Skins]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

Auctionable: ✔ Yes

Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet skins change the look and particle trail of your pet but only one skin can be active at a time!; This skin can only be applied to Rabbit pets.; Right-click on your summoned pet to apply this skin!; RARE COSMETIC
The Aquamarine Rabbit Skin is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Aquamarine Rabbit Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on April 14, 2021. Being [[Rarity|RARE]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and, unlike other Fire Sales, had an unlimited stock, with 4,586 sold.
## Usage
The Aquamarine Rabbit Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Rabbit Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

# Arachne Fragment
Aliases: Arachne Fragments
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items
[Arachne Fragment]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Combat Sack ([[Combat Sack]]); Medium Combat Sack ([[Combat Sack]]); Large Combat Sack ([[Combat Sack]])
[Shop]
Sell: 500 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ARACHNE_FRAGMENT
Tooltip: A fragment imbued with the power of Arachne.; RARE
The Arachne Fragment is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item dropped from [[Arachne]] in the [[Spider's Den]]. They are not to be confused with [[Arachne's Calling]] which are used to summon [Lv87] Arachne.
## Usage
[[Arachne Fragment]] 2 Arachne Fragments can be traded in along with 16 [[Enchanted Spider Eye]] and 16 [[Enchanted String]] at [[Shaggy]] to obtain an [[Arachne Crystal]], which can be used to summon a [Lv143] Arachne.
## Obtaining
An Arachne Fragment can be obtained by killing a [Lv87] [[Arachne]].
[[Shaggy]] rewards an Arachne Fragment as a [[Garden Visitors|Visitor]] in [[The Garden]].
## History

Arachne's Armor is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Armor|Armor Set]]. Its Tiered Bonus: Arachne's Faithful is upgraded by pieces of Arachne's Armor, [[Arachne's Necklace]], [[Arachne's Cloak]], [[Arachne's Belt]], and [[Arachne's Gloves]].

Pieces of Arachne's Armor have a 10% chance to drop from the [[Arachne|Lesser Version]] of [[Arachne]], and a 25% chance to drop from the [[Arachne|Greater Version]]. The [[Equipment]] pieces have a 2.5% chance to drop from [[Arachne's Keeper|Arachne's Keepers]].

# Arachne's Calling

[Shop]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ARACHNE_KEEPER_FRAGMENT
Tooltip: Place 4 of these at the Altar in Arachne's Sanctuary to summon her.; &quot;It's time.&quot; - Uninformed Spider; RARE
Arachne's Calling (previously Arachne's Keeper Fragment) is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that is used to summon [[Arachne]] in the [[Spider's Den]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv31]|[[Arachne's Keeper]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv8-10]|[[Dasher Spider]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.2%|
|[Lv7-9]|[[Weaver Spider]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.02%|
## Usage
Four Arachne's Calling can be placed at the altar in the [[Spider's Den]] caves to summon [[Arachne]]'s weaker form.
## Tips
- It is recommended to use an AoE weapon like the [[Flower of Truth]], [[Spirit Sceptre]], a weapon with the [[Cleave]] enchantment, or any [[Bows|bow]] to clear spiders easily in order to get these.
- If you have an Arachne's Keeper Fragment from before the 0.16.1 update, dropping it on the ground and picking it back up will convert it to an Arachne's Calling.

|Mastiff Boots ([[Mastiff Armor]])|Wolf Slayer IV (4) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|1x Boots of Growth ([[Armor of Growth]]) 4x [[Golden Tooth]] 64x [[Enchanted Diamond]]|Recipe: [[Golden Tooth]] ×4 + [[Enchanted Diamond]] x16 ×4 + [[Armor of Growth]] → [[Mastiff Armor]]|
## Bugs
- The set's ability does not trigger when killing mobs using abilities.
## Trivia
- This set is mainly to make [[Mastiff Armor]], as Armor of Growth's lack of damage stats and low boosts compared to other, cheaper armor sets makes it unfit for combat to most players.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ARTISANAL_SHORTBOW
Tooltip: Damage: +40; Shot Cooldown: 0.5s; Shortbow: Instantly shoots!; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 4.; RARE BOW
The Artisanal Shortbow is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Bows|Bow]].
## Obtaining
The Artisanal Shortbow can be purchased from the [[Weaponsmith]] for 600 [[Coins]] after the player has reached [[Combat|Combat IV (4)]].
## Usage
Bow:
Artisanal Shortbow [[Damage]]: +40
[Effects]
[Shortbow: Instantly shoots!; Shot Cooldown: 0.5s]
The Artisanal Shortbow is a shortbow, meaning it can fire arrows instantly, without needing to charge. Only right click can be held to continuously fire, but rapidly left clicking fires arrows slightly faster. The maximum rate of fire is determined by the player's [[Attack Speed]].
## Fire Rate
When left-clicking, the fire rate starts at 2.22 shots per second (9 ticks per shot) with 0 [[Attack Speed]] and increases to 5 shots per second (4 ticks per shot) with 100 [[Attack Speed]]. The increase in fire rate with [[Attack Speed]] is incremental, not continuous. The maximum possible fire rate that can be achieved by optimal left-clicking depending on the player's [[Attack Speed]]:
|Ticks per shot|[[Attack Speed]] Lowest|[[Attack Speed]] Highest|Shots per second|
|-|-|-|-|
|9 (0.45s)|< -8|11|2.22|
|7 (0.35s)|12|40|2.86|
|5 (0.25s)|41|66|4.00|
|4 (0.20s)|67|100|5.00|
Only holding right-click, the fire rate starts at 1.67 shots per second (12 ticks per shot) at 0 [[Attack Speed]] and increases to 2.5 shots per second (8 ticks per shot) above about 50 [[Attack Speed]]. It is believed that this happens because items attempt to cast their right-click abilities every 4 ticks when right-click is held continuously.
## Tips
- Early players can use this bow to do Creeper Beams and Blaze puzzle (Higher or Lower) faster as it doesn't need to be drawn.
- It is recommended to switch to using a [[Dragon Shortbow]] after completing the [[Lone Adventurer]] quest in the [[The End|End]] or [[Juju Shortbow]] later after reaching [[Enderman Slayer]] Level 5 or [[Terminator]] after reaching [[Enderman Slayer]] Level 7.

The Ascension Rope is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item found in the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Obtaining
Ascension Ropes can be found in [[Crystal Hollows|Treasure Chests]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Usage
Right clicking with an Ascension Rope while in the [[Crystal Hollows]] in a hand will teleport the player to the entrance they spawned in.
While in the [[Glacite Mineshafts]], an Ascension Rope can be used to return to the [[Dwarven Base Camp]] and receive all rewards associated with leaving a Mineshaft alive. The item is consumed on use.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ASPECT_OF_THE_END
Tooltip: Damage: +100; Strength: +100; Gemstones: []; Ability: Instant Transmission RIGHT CLICK Teleport 8 blocks ahead of you and gain +50  Speed for 3 seconds.; Mana Cost: 50; This item can be reforged!; RARE SWORD
The Aspect of the End, frequently abbreviated as AOTE, is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Swords|Sword]] unlocked at [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearl VIII (8)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Ender Pearl VIII (8) ([[Ender Pearl]])|1x [[Enchanted Diamond]] 32x [[Enchanted Eye of Ender]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Eye of Ender]] x16 ×2 + [[Enchanted Diamond]] → Aspect of the End|
## Usage
Sword:
Aspect of the End [[Damage]]: +100 [[Strength]]: +100
[Effects]
[Ability: Instant Transmission RIGHT CLICK; Teleports you 8 blocks ahead of you and you gain +50 Speed for 3 seconds.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 50]
When the ability is used, it arrests its user's momentum and teleports them 8 blocks in the direction they are looking. This teleport cannot pass through walls, including , but can pass through 1-block holes in Iron Bar walls. After teleporting, its user gains 50 [[Speed]] for 3s. The potency of this speed boost stacks with multiple uses, but each stack wears off when its individual duration ends. The Ability has no cooldown and costs 50 [[Mana]]. The ability cannot be used in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]] boss room.
It can be upgraded with [[Transmission Tuner|Transmission Tuners]].
It can be reforged to Warped with a [[Warped Stone]], granting it +165 [[Damage]] and +165 [[Strength]].
It can be improved with the [[Strong Dragon Armor]] set. With the Strong Blood Full Set Bonus, the sword gains +75 [[Damage]], +2 blocks of teleport distance, +3 seconds of [[Speed]] bonus, and +5 [[Strength]] after teleporting.
### Aspect of the Void
It can be upgraded to [[Aspect of the Void]] at [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer LVL VI (6)]], granting it +20 [[Damage]] and reducing its ability [[Mana]] cost by 5, from 50 to 45. It keeps all [[Reforging|Reforges]]/[[Enchantments]]/etc. when upgraded.
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Enderman Slayer VI (6) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|[[Aspect of the End]] 1x Aspect of the End 32x [[Null Ovoid]]|Recipe: [[Null Ovoid]] x8 ×4 + Aspect of the End → [[Aspect of the Void]]|
### Etherwarp Upgrade
It can be combined with an [[Etherwarp Conduit]] with an [[Etherwarp Merger]]. This grants it the [[Etherwarp Conduit]]'s long-range teleport ability when right clicking while sneaking. Note that when adding a Conduit, reforges/enchantments/etc., will not be kept (this is a bug)!
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Enderman Slayer VII (7) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|1x [[Etherwarp Merger]] 1x [[Etherwarp Conduit]] [[Aspect of the End]] 1x Aspect of the End|Recipe: Aspect of the End + [[Etherwarp Merger]] + [[Etherwarp Conduit]] → Aspect of the End|
## Trivia
- Using the sword's ability just before hitting the ground will cancel any fall damage.
- If it is reforged away from Warped, it would retain its status as a Dungeon item.
- When reforged to Warped, it and its upgraded form, the [[Aspect of the Void]], were the only non-accessory Dungeon items which could not be upgraded with stars.
  - This was later removed.
- Using the [[Warped Stone|Warped]] Reforge, a max level [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Enderman Pet]], a full set of [[Strong Dragon Armor]] and maxed on [[Hot Potato Book|Hot Potato Books]] and [[Fuming Potato Book|Fuming Potato Books]], the Aspect of the End will grant +420 [[Damage]] and +295 [[Strength]] after using the Instant Transmission Ability.
- The item ability will always teleport the player to an integer Y coordinate, and 0.5 + an integer X and Z [[Coordinates]], which means that every time the player uses the Ability, they will always end up in the middle of a block.
- The item Ability Instant Transmission is likely a reference to the technique used in Dragon Ball with the same name.
- Prior to the 0.19.10 update, it was better to reforge the Aspect of the End to [[Reforging|Heroic]] for more [[Intelligence]] once the player had a better weapon to use for damage, but now the [[Reforging|Warped]] reforge gives equal [[Intelligence]] (more when [[Recombobulator 3000|recombobulated]]) and higher [[Damage]] and [[Strength]].
## History

|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Aspect of the End as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Aspect of the End.|
|Jun 20, 2019|0.3|+20 [[Damage]] (from +80 to +100).|
|Jun 20, 2019|0.3|+50 [[Strength]] (from +50 to +100).|
|Jul 1, 2020|0.7.11|Instant Transmission no longer teleports the player through blocks.|

|May 31, 2021|0.11.5|Can now be upgraded with [[Etherwarp Conduit]] and [[Etherwarp Merger]].|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Aspect of the End as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chests
The Balloon Snake can be obtained from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor I]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor I ([[The Catacombs - Floor I]])|Diamond|50,000 [[Coins]]|1.1495% (1 in 87 runs)|1.9774% (1 in 50.6 runs)|170|2|
|Floor I ([[The Catacombs - Floor I]])|Emerald|100,000 [[Coins]]|1.4085% (1 in 71 runs)|2.8883% (1 in 34.6 runs)|170|2|
|Floor I ([[The Catacombs - Floor I]])|Obsidian|250,000 [[Coins]]|7.6911% (1 in 13 runs)|10.8403% (1 in 9.2 runs)|170|4|
|Floor I ([[The Catacombs - Floor I]]) (Master Mode)|Diamond|50,000 [[Coins]]|1.9355% (1 in 51.7 runs)|3.4553% (1 in 28.9 runs)|170|3|
|Floor I ([[The Catacombs - Floor I]]) (Master Mode)|Emerald|100,000 [[Coins]]|3.9668% (1 in 25.2 runs)|5.858% (1 in 17.1 runs)|170|3|
|Floor I ([[The Catacombs - Floor I]]) (Master Mode)|Obsidian|250,000 [[Coins]]|6.7296% (1 in 14.9 runs)|9.4552% (1 in 10.6 runs)|170|3|
## Usage
Necklace:
Balloon Snake [[Health]]: +10 [[Intelligence]]: +10
[Effects]
[Grants Jump Boost II (Jump Boost Potion) while equipped]
The Balloon Snake grants permanent Jump Boost II when equipped.
## Essence Upgrading
Essence Required; [[Wither Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|5 Essence|5 Essence|
|✪✪|10 Essence|15 Essence|
|✪✪✪|20 Essence|35 Essence|
|✪✪✪✪|35 Essence; 10,000 [[Coins]]|70 Essence; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|50 Essence; 25,000 [[Coins]]|120 Essence; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- Since Jump Boost is disabled in the [[Catacombs]], the Balloon Snake is useful for better mobility.
- Due to the fact that the [[Bone Necklace]] provides no offensive stats that are useful for non-[[Bows|Bow]] damage once the player has reached 100 [[Crit Chance]], while the Balloon Snake provides [[Intelligence]], the Balloon Snake provides slightly more damage for Left-Click [[Mage]] in [[Dungeons]] than the Bone Necklace when both are reforged to [[Reforging|Menacing]] and [[Recombobulator 3000|recombobulated]], since both gain the same [[Crit Damage]] from the reforge.
## Trivia
- The Balloon Snake is the only piece of Equipment to grant a [[Potions|Potion Effect]].
## History

|Sep 7, 2023|Added Balloon Snake.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

The Bat Talisman is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Accessories|Accessory]], which has a rare chance to drop from killing [[Bat (disambiguation)|Bats]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv1]|[[Bat (Private Island)]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
The Bat Talisman has a 1% (1/100) chance to be dropped from killing [Lv3] Bats spawned by a [[Floating Crystals|Bat Crystal]] on a [[Roofed Forest Island]] placed on the player's [[Private Island]].
Bats will spawn only at night, and only one crystal of this type can be placed on an island at once.
## Usage
Accessory:
Bat Talisman [[Health]]: +1 [[Intelligence]]: +1 [[Speed]]: +1
### Shop Purchase
During the [[Spooky Festival]], the Bat Talisman can be upgraded into the [[Bat Ring]] by the [[Fear Mongerer]]:
||Name|Rarity|Stat|Cost|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Bat Ring]]|[[Bat Ring]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|+3 HP ([[Health]]), +2 Spd ([[Speed]]), +2 Int ([[Intelligence]])|[[Bat Talisman]] 1 Bat Talisman, 64 (1 stack) [[Green Candy]]|
## Trivia
- If the player has a Bat Talisman in their inventory and receives a message that a second talisman has dropped, they will not receive the second talisman until the player moves the first talisman out of their inventory.

## Usage
Only 1 Beacon can be placed on a Private Island.
In order to power the Beacon the player must fuel it with [[Power Crystal|Power Crystals]] or [[Scorched Power Crystal|Scorched Power Crystals]], granting 48 hours of power per crystal. Breaking a Beacon will cause any fuel to be lost. There is no maximum amount of time that the Beacon can be fueled for at once, other than the 64-bit integer limit of 2.923e8 years (9,223,372,036,854,775,807 milliseconds).
The Beacon gives a chosen stat to the player on all different SkyBlock Islands. Using a [[Scorched Power Crystal]] increases the given stat boost by +20%. The Beacon can be used to boost all [[Minions]]. Using a [[Scorched Power Crystal]] gives an additional +1% to the minion buff.

## Trivia
- Powering any Beacon will grant the player the [[Achievements|"Beaconator 2.0" Achievement]].
- Prior to update 0.11.1, the minion buffs given by the Beacon were 10x what they were meant to be (i.e. a Beacon I would give a 20% minion buff instead of 2%, and so on.)
- Contrary to popular belief, the minion buff does indeed apply to offline calculations.
- [[Magic Find]] is the only buff that affects the [[Rift Dimension]]. [[Intelligence]] does not.

The Beaconmite Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Beacon Zealot]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Beacon Tuning
A Beaconmite Shard has a 10% (1 in 10 rolls) chance to obtained from tuning a [[Moonglade Beacon]] or [[Torrhus Beacon]].
### Fusion
A Beaconmite Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Beaconmite Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Beacon Zealot]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Beacon Zealot ([[Attributes]]); The effects of Galatea's beacons are +10%–100% stronger on you. Gain a +0.1%–1% chance to find Signal Enhancers ([[Signal Enhancer]]) in [[Tree Gifts]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Beaconmite Shard has the following description:
- These bugs are drawn to the concentrated energy of beacons, feeding off the light and using it to fuel their rapid, darting movements.

Item ID: BEADY_EYES
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Beady reforge when combined with a Vacuum.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Damage  Intelligence; Beady Bonus Grants +100 Farming Fortune towards  Pests.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 15.; RARE REFORGE STONE
Beady Eyes is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Beady]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Vacuums]]. It grants [[Damage]] and [[Intelligence]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv1]|[[Fly]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|3%* ([[Pests]])|
## Usage
### Reforging
Beady Eyes can be combined with a [[Vacuums|Vacuum]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Beady]] reforge, to grant [[Damage]] and [[Intelligence]], which scale with the Vacuum's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XV (15)]]. A Vacuum that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Beady]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Beady Bonus, which makes the item deal 100 [[Farming Fortune]] against [[Pest (Mob Type)|Pests]].

## Tips
- If one is able to oneshot [[Pests]], with a combination of 5 [[Bookworm's Favorite Book]], [Lv100] [[Hedgehog Pet]] and the [[Attributes|Insect Power]] attribute obtained from syphoning [[Praying Mantis Shard|Praying Mantis Shards]], Beady is preferable over the more used [[Reforging|Buzzing]], due to its 100 [[Farming Fortune]] against Pests.

The Beagle Hound Skin is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Beagle Hound Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on August 24, 2021. Being [[Rarity|RARE]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 5,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Beagle Hound Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Hound Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.
